- [Okta](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/authentication/single-sign-on-saml/okta.md): Use Okta as a SAML SSO provider to let your users log into Harness with their Okta credentials.

- [Keycloak](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/authentication/single-sign-on-saml/keycloak.md): Connect Keycloak to Harness with SAML to sign in with existing credentials, and optionally sync groups for access control.

- [Define failure strategies for stages and steps](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/failure-handling/define-a-failure-strategy-on-stages-and-steps.md): A failure strategy defines how your stages and steps handle different failure conditions.
- [Rollback pipelines](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/failure-handling/define-a-failure-strategy-for-pipelines.md): Configure pipeline rollback to roll back the entire pipeline upon stage or step failure.
- [Mark pipeline as failed](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/failure-handling/mark-as-failed-pipeline.md): Mark a pipeline as failed during execution to stop all running and queued stages.
- [Mark stage as failed](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/failure-handling/mark-as-failed.md): Mark a stage as failed during pipeline execution to abort running steps and trigger failure strategies.
- [Mark step as failed](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/failure-handling/mark-step-as-failed.md): Mark a step as failed during pipeline execution to abort it and trigger failure strategies.
- [Abort a pipeline, stage, or step](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/failure-handling/abort-pipeline.md): Abort pipeline, stage, or step execution.
- [Retry failed executions from any stage](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/failure-handling/resume-pipeline-deployments.md): Resume pipeline executions from any successful or failed stage to avoid rerunning the entire pipeline.
- [Timeout settings](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/failure-handling/timeout-settings.md): Configure timeout settings for steps, stages, and pipelines to prevent excessive execution times.
- [Fail fast pipeline](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/failure-handling/fast-fail.md): Enable fail fast behavior to immediately fail pipelines when any step or stage in a parallel group fails.
- [Conditional executions for stages and steps](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/step-skip-condition-settings.md): You can define conditional execution settings for stages and steps to control when they run based on pipeline status and JEXL conditions.

- [Looping strategies](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/looping-strategies/looping-strategies-matrix-repeat-and-parallelism.md): Looping strategies include matrix, repeat, and parallelism strategies.
- [Best practices for looping strategies](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/looping-strategies/best-practices-for-looping-strategies.md): Recommended practices for implementing looping strategies in production pipelines, and what fails when teams skip them.
- [Matrix strategy examples](https://developer.harness.io/harness-ai/use-harness-platform/pipelines/looping-strategies/additional-matrix-examples.md): Examples and patterns for matrix strategies in Harness pipelines, including basic matrices, nested loops, runtime input, and complex JSON values.

## Harness Platform

- [Platform](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/readme.md)
- [Overview](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/new-to-harness-platform/overview.md): Learn key concepts and overview of the Harness Platform including accounts, organizations, projects, delegates, connectors, pipelines, and RBAC.

- [Authentication](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/use-harness-platform/authentication.md): Configure authentication methods, password policies, session timeouts, and audit login events in Harness.
- [Single sign-on with SAML](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/use-harness-platform/authentication/single-sign-on-saml.md): Overview of SAML-based single sign-on (SSO) in Harness, including key concepts, supported formats, and SCIM integration settings.
- [Okta](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/use-harness-platform/authentication/single-sign-on-saml/okta.md): Use Okta as a SAML SSO provider to let your users log into Harness with their Okta credentials.

* [Secret Types](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/in-harness-3.0/secrets/types.md): Harness supports two types of secrets; Text Secrets for inline string values and File Secrets for uploaded file content. * [Pipelines](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview.md): Overview of Harness pipelines, how to navigate to them, the pipelines and executions listing views, and two ways to create a pipeline.
* [Pipeline Studio](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/pipeline-studio.md): Overview of Pipeline Studio, the Visual and YAML editors, the Configure pipeline panel, and the pipeline navigation tabs.
* [Input sets](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/input-sets.md): Save and reuse runtime input values across pipeline runs using input sets and overlays.
* [Pipeline settings](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/pipeline-settings.md): Configure per-pipeline settings including inputs, delegate, notifications, clone behavior, timeout duration, selective stage execution, and rerun behavior.
* [Stage](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview.md): Stages are the primary execution blocks within a Harness pipeline. Each stage groups related steps and has its own runtime, settings, and configuration.
* [Stage types](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview/blank-canvas.md): A Blank canvas stage starts empty so you can add any combination of step types manually.
* [Build](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview/blank-canvas/build.md): A Build stage pre-configures a stage for compiling, testing, and validating code.
* [Deploy](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview/blank-canvas/deploy.md): A Deploy stage pre-configures a stage for deploying services to environments.
* [Template](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview/blank-canvas/template.md): A Template stage applies a pre-built stage definition from the Harness template library, inheriting all steps and settings defined in the template.
* [Chained pipeline](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview/blank-canvas/chained-pipeline.md): A Chained pipeline stage runs another Harness pipeline as a stage within the current pipeline.
* [Serial group](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview/blank-canvas/serial-group.md): A Serial group stage is a container that holds multiple stages running one after another in sequence.
* [Parallel group](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview/blank-canvas/parallel-group.md): A Parallel group stage is a container that holds multiple stages running simultaneously.
* [Inputs & outputs](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview/inputs-and-outputs.md): Use stage-level inputs, outputs, and environment variables to pass data into a stage, export values to downstream stages, and inject configuration into steps.
* [Steps](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview-1.md): Steps are the individual units of work inside a stage. Each step executes a specific task: running a script, building an image, deploying a service, waiting for approval, and more.
* [Group](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview-1/group.md): A Group step organizes multiple steps into a named serial container. Steps inside the group run sequentially, and the group itself supports timeout, on-failure, strategy, and conditional execution.
* [Parallel](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview-1/parallel.md): A Parallel step group runs multiple steps simultaneously. Use it to cut wall-clock time by executing independent tasks at the same time.
* [Execution controls](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/strategy.md): Use a looping strategy to run a stage or step multiple times; iterate a fixed number of times with a for loop, loop conditionally with a while loop, or fan out across input combinations with a matrix.
* [Concurrency](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/strategy/concurrency.md): Limit how many strategy instances run simultaneously when a stage or step uses a matrix, for loop, or while loop strategy.
* [On failure](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/strategy/on-failure.md): Configure what Harness does when a stage or step fails — abort, retry, ignore, or mark as success.
* [Conditional execution](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/strategy/conditional-execution.md): Skip a stage or step at runtime by setting a condition that must evaluate to true for it to run.
* [Timeout](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/strategy/timeout.md): Set a maximum run time for a stage or step. Harness terminates it if it exceeds the timeout duration.
* [Execution logs](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/overview-2.md): Understand the execution detail view, how to read stage and step logs, and how to re-run a pipeline.
* [YAML reference](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/overview/yaml-reference.md): Complete YAML reference for Harness v1 pipelines: every key, its type, and a minimal example.
* [Variables & Expressions](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/variables-and-expressions.md)
* [Fixed values, runtime inputs, and expressions](https://developer.harness.io/harness-platform/3.0/harness-platform-resources/variables-and-expressions/runtime-inputs.md): You can change the input type for most settings in Harness.

## Code Repository

- [Code Repository](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/readme.md): Access Harness Code Repository documentation
- [Overview and key concepts](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/new-to-harness-code/overview.md): Learn about Harness Code Repository and the source code management concepts it builds on.
- [Get started with Harness Code Repository](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/new-to-harness-code/onboarding-guide.md): A self-service onboarding guide that takes you from an empty project to repositories connected to pipelines and governed by access controls.
- [Manage Repositories](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories.md)
- [Create repositories](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories/create-repo.md): Create repositories in Harness Code
- [Import repositories](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories/import-repo.md): Import repositories in Harness Code
- [Fork repositories](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories/fork-repo.md): Fork repositories in Harness Code to create independent copies for contributions and experimentation.
- [Mirror repositories](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories/mirror.md): Create a CI pipeline that mirrors branch and tag changes between two repositories, in one direction or both.
- [Enable rules](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories/rules.md): Set up branch rules, tag rules, push rules, and CODEOWNERS to govern repositories in Harness Code Repository.
- [Resource tags](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories/resource-tags.md): Add tags to repositories, filter the repository list by tag, and control access with tag-based RBAC.
- [Enable security](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories/security.md): Enable secret scanning, vulnerability scanning, committer email verification, OPA policies, and audit logging for Harness Code repositories.
- [Configure webhooks](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories/webhooks.md): Set up webhooks for your Harness Code repositories.
- [Archive repositories](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories/archive-repo.md): Archive repositories in Harness Code
- [Favorite repositories](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories/favorite-repo.md): Mark and filter favorite repositories in Harness Code
- [Delete repositories](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/manage-repositories/delete-repo.md): Delete repositories in Harness Code
- [Collaborate & Develop](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/collaborate-and-develop.md)
- [Clone a repository](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/collaborate-and-develop/clone-repos.md): Clone Harness Code repositories over HTTPS or SSH, and use partial and shallow clone to reduce transfer size.
- [Branch](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/collaborate-and-develop/branch.md): Create, switch, compare, protect, and delete branches in Harness Code repositories.
- [Commit](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/collaborate-and-develop/commit.md): Create and manage commits in your Harness Code repositories.
- [Sign commits](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/collaborate-and-develop/signing-commits.md): Use GPG or SSH keys to sign commits and verify authorship in Harness Code Repository.
- [Tag](https://developer.harness.io/code-repository/2.0/use-harness-code/collaborate-and-develop/tag.md): Create and manage tags in your Harness Code repositories.

* [Deployments](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/deployments.md): Deploy application and infrastructure changes safely with Harness Deployments
* [Overview](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/new-to-deployments/overview.md): Deploy services, infrastructure, and databases to any environment using automated pipelines and governed execution.
* [Get started with Kubernetes deployments](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/new-to-deployments/get-started.md): Deploy a container image to a Kubernetes cluster using Harness CD in seven steps.
* [What's Supported](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/new-to-deployments/cd-integrations.md): Supported deployment targets, manifest stores, and artifact sources for Harness Deployments.
* [Kubernetes](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes.md)
* [Overview](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/overview.md): Deploy container images to a Kubernetes cluster using Harness CD.
* [Kubernetes services](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/kubernetes-services.md): Configure Kubernetes services in Harness, manifests, artifact sources, override files, and sidecars.
* [Kubernetes infrastructure](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/kubernetes-infrastructure.md): Create Harness environments and Kubernetes infrastructure definitions to target your cluster for deployment.
* [Deployment Strategies](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/kubernetes-deployment-strategies.md)
* [Kubernetes rolling deployment](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/kubernetes-deployment-strategies/rolling.md): Deploy a new version to all pods incrementally using a rolling update, with automatic rollback on failure.
* [Kubernetes canary deployment](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/kubernetes-deployment-strategies/canary.md): Deploy a canary version of your application alongside the stable version, validate it, then promote with a full rolling deploy.
* [Kubernetes blue-green deployment](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/kubernetes-deployment-strategies/blue-green.md): Deploy a new version to a stage environment, validate it, then swap service selectors to route production traffic to the new version.
* [Blank canvas Kubernetes deployment](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/kubernetes-deployment-strategies/blank-canvas.md): Build a Kubernetes stage from scratch using the Apply step, without a managed rolling, canary, or blue-green strategy.
* [Step Library](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library.md)
* [Kubernetes Apply](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-apply.md): Apply Kubernetes manifests directly to the cluster without using a managed release strategy.
* [Kubernetes Canary Deploy](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-canary-deploy.md): Deploy a subset of pods with the new version alongside the stable version for validation before a full rollout.
* [Kubernetes Blue Green Swap Services Selectors](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-blue-green-swap-services.md): Switch Kubernetes service selectors to route production traffic to the new pod set after a blue-green deploy.
* [Kubernetes Rolling Deploy](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-rolling-deploy.md): Prepare manifests, apply them to the cluster, and wait for all pods to reach steady state.
* [Kubernetes Delete](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-delete.md): Delete Kubernetes resources by name, manifest path, or release name.
* [Kubernetes Steady State Check](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-steady-state-check.md): Wait for Kubernetes workloads to reach a healthy running state after a deployment or scale operation.
* [Kubernetes Rolling Rollback](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-rolling-rollback.md): Re-apply the previous release manifests to roll back a failed rolling or canary deployment.
* [Kubernetes Diff](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-diff.md): Compare the current state of the cluster with the desired state defined in your manifests.
* [Kubernetes Traffic Shift step](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-traffic-shift.md): Shift traffic between service versions using Istio VirtualServices or Kubernetes Gateway API HTTPRoutes.
* [Kubernetes Scale](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-scale.md): Scale a Kubernetes workload to a specific number of instances or a percentage of current instances.
* [Kubernetes Blue Green Deploy](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-blue-green-deploy.md): Deploy the new version to the stage pod set and wait for it to reach steady state before a traffic swap.
* [Kubernetes Patch](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-patch.md): Modify a specific Kubernetes workload in place without redeploying the full manifest.
* [Kubernetes Dry Run](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-dry-run.md): Validate Kubernetes manifests against the cluster without applying changes.
* [Kubernetes Rollout](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-rollout.md): Run kubectl rollout subcommands against Kubernetes workloads identified by name, manifest path, or release name.
* [Kubernetes Canary Delete](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-canary-delete.md): Remove the canary workload after validation or on rollback.
* [Kubernetes Blue Green Stage Scale Down](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/kubernetes/step-library/k8s-blue-green-stage-scale-down.md): Clean up the old pod set after a successful blue-green swap to free cluster resources.
* [Helm](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm.md)
* [Overview](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/overview.md): Deploy Helm charts to a Kubernetes cluster using Harness CD.
* [Helm services](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/helm-services.md): Configure Helm services in Harness: chart sources, values files, artifact sources, sidecars, and advanced options.
* [Helm infrastructure](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/helm-infrastructure.md): Create Harness environments and Native Helm infrastructure definitions to target your cluster for Helm deployments.
* [Deployment Strategies](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/helm-deployment-strategies.md)
* [Helm basic deployment](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/helm-deployment-strategies/basic.md): Deploy a Helm chart to all instances in a single phase using helm upgrade --install.
* [Helm canary deployment](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/helm-deployment-strategies/canary.md): Deploy a Helm chart to a subset of instances using a separate canary release, validate, then promote to full deployment.
* [Helm blue-green deployment](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/helm-deployment-strategies/blue-green.md): Deploy a Helm chart to a stage service alongside the primary release, then swap traffic between them.
* [Blank canvas Helm deployment](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/helm-deployment-strategies/blank-canvas.md): Build a Helm stage from scratch using the Helm Basic Deploy step, without a managed canary or blue-green strategy.
* [Step Library](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/step-library.md)
* [Helm Basic Deploy](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/step-library/helm-basic-deploy.md): Run helm upgrade --install against the target cluster and wait for all workloads to reach steady state.
* [Helm Canary Deploy](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/step-library/helm-canary-deploy.md): Install a separate canary Helm release alongside the stable release with a controlled instance count or percentage.
* [Helm Canary Delete](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/step-library/helm-canary-delete.md): Uninstall the canary Helm release after validation or on rollback.
* [Helm Blue Green Deploy](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/step-library/helm-blue-green-deploy.md): Deploy the new chart version to the stage service and wait for it to reach steady state before a traffic swap.
* [Helm Blue Green Swap](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/step-library/helm-blue-green-swap.md): Switch service selectors to route production traffic to the new workload after a blue-green deploy.
* [Helm Rollback](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/step-library/helm-rollback.md): Revert a Helm release to a previous revision after a failed deployment.
* [Helm Delete](https://developer.harness.io/continuous-delivery/3.0/use-deployments/helm/step-library/helm-delete.md): Uninstall a Helm release and remove all associated Kubernetes resources from the cluster.
